Session Handoff — Reading Center rebuilt as a standalone Spreeder copy: reader, library and Profile done; the remaining features still need to be tested against real Spreeder
Where it started
Joshua pasted the previous handoff, in which the Reading Center build was paused at Step 10 inside LSAT U. He then changed course twice:

* First, put it in a Teacher Workspace "Tools" room.
* Then, build it as an entirely separate web app first and integrate it into LSAT U later. "Assign" will mean granting a student access to the whole tool.

He then required it to match Spreeder exactly in colours, layout and every feature's behaviour, using reverse-engineer-anything as the source of truth. His last two messages, both interrupted, were: "do all of the features and option working and mirror it /reverse-engineer-anything does it flow perfectly and such in the same way as spreeder".
Decisions locked + what shipped

* Standalone app. It lives at `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os-projects\reading-center`: Vite + React + TS + Tailwind, a local git repo with no remote, and data saved in the browser (`src/features/reading-center/lib/store.ts`). Commits: `d2af782` (initial), `441536b` (Spreeder rebuild), `e41d2c2` (fixes for hidden tabs, the Flow mask and chart animation).
* Tools room reverted on the LSAT U branch `feat/reading-center`. Revert commit `d5bbd69b` is in the worktree `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os-projects\lsatu-reading-center`. Earlier commits on that branch, unpushed: Step 10 fix `8ff3641c`, steps 11–17 (`3e924766`, `2f88c0d1`, `053212ed`, `c3472072`, `dbbfa1dd`, `8ab5ace4`).
* The LSAT U copy is out of date. `src/features/reading-center/` on that branch is now far behind the standalone app. Integrating means porting the standalone code across, not merging.
* Reconstruction spec. `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os\research\2026-10-13-spreeder-reconstruction-spec.md` replaces the visual guidance in the old build spec. It covers the palette for each theme, every screen's measurements, the text model, layout, the timing formula and the dialogs. Section 7 is build status and gaps.
* Engine ported from Spreeder's shipped code (`lib/spreeder.ts`):
   * whitespace collapsed, no paragraphs
   * sentence sections of about 2,000–2,400 characters
   * line building by measured width and Words Per Line
   * the `evenChunkDelays`, variability and punctuation (×2) rules
   * remaining time and progress formulas
* Worker timer (`lib/workerTimer.ts`). Like Spreeder, it uses a Web Worker because `setTimeout` is clamped to 1 s in hidden tabs.
* Flow differs from Spreeder on purpose. Spreeder's mask uses `timeDiff/m`, which is buggy and shows almost nothing. Ours is the intended left-to-right reveal.
* Brand assets not copied. No Spreeder logo, cover art or icon font. Ionicons (npm `ionicons@7.4.0`) and Material Icons via Google Fonts stand in.
* Goal recommendation uses Spreeder's real logic (`lib/goals.ts`): reading-test WPM, then a piecewise curve with max 2000.
* Not built (need a service): Look Up Word, Add to Wordela Word List, AI Book Summary, AI Generate, the 20,504-book catalogue, Courses content, share, Sign Out.
* Built from inference (not observed in Spreeder): the filled bookmark list, the Tag, Edit Image, Delete and Edit Profile dialogs, the reading-test text picker, charts with data, the Light-theme sidenav colours, the speedometer artwork.

65	### Text model (shipped code)
66	- Whitespace collapsed to single spaces; em dash → "-"; **no paragraphs**.
67	- Sections: sentences (regex `[^.!?]+([.!?"”]+|$)`) accumulated until ~2000 chars; a sentence that would push past 2400 is split at a word boundary. Sentence ends after "Dr/Mr/Mrs/Ms" or a single letter do not close a section.
68	- Words = split(" "); id 1-based; charPosition.
69	- Progress % = round((sectionIndex + posInSection/sectionLen) / sections * 100) (1 when on the last word of a section).
70	- Remaining = time from current word to end: "N hour(s) and M minute(s)", "Less than a minute".
71	
72	### Layout (Serial / Flow / Highlight / Scroll)
73	- Words are measured in the font; a line takes words while `x + width + space <= stageWidth - 40` **and** words < Words Per Line.
74	- Serial: slide = Number of Lines lines, block vertically centred; Center Text centres each line, else x=20.
75	- Flow: forced 1 line, spacing 1, left-aligned x=20; each chunk revealed by a bg-coloured mask sweeping left→right across the window over the chunk's duration (blank when paused at chunk start).
76	- Highlight: page = floor((height-20)/fontSize) lines, left x=20, all visible in unhighlighted colour; current group (Number of Lines lines) drawn in font colour, not bold.
77	- Scroll: same page, only current group visible at its own y; previous group hidden; new page starts at top.
78	- Font size clamp 8..74. Line spacing multiplies fontSize.
79	
80	### Timing (shipped code)
81	- Section total T = round(words / wpm * 60000). Chunks n. Even delay = T / (n - 1 + f), last = even*f where f = min(lastChunkLines / numberOfLines, 1).
82	- Speed Variability: delay = round(T/n) * (chunkChars + (notLast?1:0)) / (sectionChars/n).
83	- Punctuation Pauses: delay *= 2 if chunk text contains any of `. ? ! : ; - – ,` (any group size).
84	- WPM clamp 50..5000, step 10 (keys ↑/+ and ↓/-), rounded to 10.
85

10	/** Spreeder themes the whole app from the reading theme. */
11	export function useAppTheme(): void {
12	  const theme: ReaderTheme = usePreferences().data?.theme ?? "system";
13	  useEffect(() => {
14	    const mq = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)");
15	    const apply = () => {
16	      const resolved = theme === "system" ? (mq.matches ? "dark" : "light") : theme;
17	      document.documentElement.dataset.rcTheme = resolved;
18	    };
19	    apply();
20	    mq.addEventListener("change", apply);
21	    return () => mq.removeEventListener("change", apply);
22	  }, [theme]);
23	}

28	  { to: RC_COURSES_PATH, label: "Courses", icon: "school" },
29	  { to: RC_PROFILE_PATH, label: "Profile", icon: "person" },
30	];

44	              <Icon name="menu" size={24} />

54	            >
55	              <Icon name="addCircle" size={22} className="ml-[8px]" />
56	              <span className="ml-[30px] leading-8">Add Text</span>
57	            </button>

83	                    <Icon name={n.icon} size={18} className="ml-[16px]" />
84	                    <span className="ml-[17px]" style={{ color: isActive ? "var(--rc-nav-text)" : undefined }}>{n.label}</span>
85	                  </>
86	                )}
87	              </NavLink>
88	            ))}
89	          </nav>
90	        </aside>
91	      )}
92	      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
93	        {!navOpen && (
94	          <button type="button" aria-label="Show menu" onClick={() => setNavOpen(true)} className="fixed left-[17px] top-[10px] z-30 flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text)" }}>
95	            <Icon name="menu" size={24} />
96	          </button>

105	function AddTextMenu({ onClose, onPick }: { onClose: () => void; onPick: (s: "paste" | "import") => void }): React.ReactElement {
106	  useEffect(() => {
107	    const close = (e: MouseEvent) => !(e.target as HTMLElement).closest("[data-rc-addmenu]") && onClose();
108	    window.addEventListener("mousedown", close);
109	    return () => window.removeEventListener("mousedown", close);
110	  }, [onClose]);
111	  const item = (icon: IconName, label: string, onClick?: () => void, title?: string) => (
112	    <button type="button" className="rc-menu-item h-12 text-sm font-medium disabled:opacity-40" onClick={onClick} disabled={!onClick} title={title}>
113	      <Icon name={icon} size={20} /> {label}
114	    </button>
115	  );
116	  return createPortal(
117	    <div data-rc-addmenu className="rc-menu fixed left-[18px] top-[125px] z-50 w-[190px] py-1">
118	      {item("copy", "Paste Text", () => onPick("paste"))}
119	      {item("documentText", "Import Document", () => onPick("import"))}
120	      {item("book", "AI Book Summary", undefined, "Needs an AI service; not available in this version")}
121	      {item("sparkles", "AI Generate", undefined, "Needs an AI service; not available in this version")}
122	    </div>,
123	    document.body,
124	  );
125	}

127	/** Spreeder top-right bar: book search box and account button. */
128	export function TopBar({ query, onQuery, placeholder }: { query?: string; onQuery?: (q: string) => void; placeholder?: string }): React.ReactElement {
129	  const navigate = useNavigate();
130	  return (
131	    <div className="flex h-[50px] items-center justify-end gap-3 pr-[44px]">
132	      {onQuery && (

134	          <input value={query} onChange={(e) => onQuery(e.target.value)} placeholder={placeholder} aria-label="Search passages" className="min-w-0 flex-1 bg-transparent font-medium outline-none placeholder:text-[var(--rc-text)]" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text)" }} />
135	          <Icon name="search" size={18} />
136	        </label>
137	      )}

139	        <Icon name="personCircle" size={34} />
140	      </button>
141	    </div>
142	  );
143	}

271	/** Ionic action sheet Spreeder opens on a word click. Look Up Word / Wordela lists are not built yet. */
272	export function WordActionSheet({ word, onClose, onBookmark }: { word: Word | null; onClose: () => void; onBookmark: (w: Word) => void }): React.ReactElement | null {
273	  if (!word) return null;
274	  const header = word.text.replace(/[^\w\s'-]/g, "").replace(/'s$/i, "").trim();
275	  return createPortal(
276	    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end justify-center" style={{ background: "rgba(0,0,0,.32)" }} onMouseDown={(e) => e.target === e.currentTarget && onClose()}>
277	      <div role="dialog" aria-label={header} className="w-full max-w-[500px] pb-2" style={{ background: "var(--rc-menu)", color: "var(--rc-text)", boxShadow: "0 -2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.2)" }}>
278	        <div className="flex h-14 items-center px-4 text-base" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }}>{header}</div>
279	        <button type="button" className="rc-menu-item h-[52px] text-base" onClick={() => onBookmark(word)}>
280	          <Icon name="bookmark" size={24} style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }} /> Bookmark/Note
281	        </button>
282	        <button type="button" className="rc-menu-item h-[52px] text-base" onClick={onClose}>
283	          <MIcon name="cancel" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }} /> Cancel
284	        </button>
285	      </div>
286	    </div>,
287	    document.body,
288	  );
289	}

1	import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
2	import { createPortal } from "react-dom";
3	import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
4	import { useDeletePassage, usePassages, useProgressList, useSaveProgress, useUpsertPassage } from "../hooks/useReadingCenterData";
5	import { buildTextModel } from "../lib/spreeder";
6	import { rcReaderPath, RC_LIBRARY_PATH } from "../lib/routes";
7	import type { Passage, Progress } from "../lib/types";
8	import { Icon, MIcon, type IconName } from "../components/Icon";
9	import { TopBar } from "../components/AppShell";
10	import { AddTextScreen } from "../components/AddText";
11	import { ViewPanel } from "../components/ViewPanel";
12	import { Modal, DialogHeader } from "../components/spreeder-ui";
13	
14	type ShelfKey = "recent" | "added" | "progress";
15	const SHELF_TITLES: Record<ShelfKey, string> = { recent: "Recent", added: "Added By Me", progress: "In Progress" };
16	
17	export default function LibraryPage(): React.ReactElement {
18	  const { tag } = useParams<{ tag?: string }>();
19	  const passages = usePassages().data ?? [];
20	  const progress = useProgressList().data ?? [];
21	  const saveProgress = useSaveProgress();
22	  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
23	  const [viewAll, setViewAll] = useState<ShelfKey | null>(null);
24	  const [menu, setMenu] = useState<{ passage: Passage; x: number; y: number; shelf: ShelfKey } | null>(null);
25	  const [action, setAction] = useState<{ kind: "view" | "image" | "edit" | "tag" | "delete"; passage: Passage } | null>(null);
26	  const navigate = useNavigate();
27	
28	  const prog = useMemo(() => new Map<string, Progress>(progress.map((p) => [p.passage_id, p])), [progress]);
29	  const visible = passages.filter((p) => p.is_published && (!tag || (p.tags ?? []).includes(tag)));
30	
31	  const shelves: Record<ShelfKey, Passage[]> = useMemo(() => {
32	    const recent = visible
33	      .filter((p) => prog.has(p.id) && !prog.get(p.id)!.hidden_from_recent)
34	      .sort((a, b) => prog.get(b.id)!.last_opened_at.localeCompare(prog.get(a.id)!.last_opened_at));
35	    const added = [...visible].sort((a, b) => b.created_at.localeCompare(a.created_at));
36	    const inProgress = visible.filter((p) => {
37	      const pr = prog.get(p.id);
38	      return !!pr && pr.word_index > 0 && !pr.completed_at;
39	    });
40	    return { recent, added, progress: inProgress };
41	  }, [visible, prog]);
42	
43	  const needle = query.trim().toLowerCase();
44	  const results = needle ? visible.filter((p) => p.title.toLowerCase().includes(needle) || (p.author ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(needle)) : null;
45	
46	  const card = (p: Passage, shelf: ShelfKey) => (
47	    <PassageCard key={`${shelf}:${p.id}`} passage={p} onMenu={(x, y) => setMenu({ passage: p, x, y, shelf })} />
48	  );
49	
50	  return (
51	    <div className="min-h-screen">
52	      <TopBar query={query} onQuery={setQuery} placeholder={`Searching ${visible.length} ${visible.length === 1 ? "text" : "texts"}..`} />
53	      <div className="px-5 pb-10">
54	        {tag && (
55	          <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-lg font-bold">
56	            <Link to={RC_LIBRARY_PATH} aria-label="All texts" className="flex"><MIcon name="arrow_back" /></Link>
57	            <Icon name="pricetag" size={18} /> {tag}
58	          </div>
59	        )}
60	        {results ? (
61	          <Shelf title={`Results (${results.length})`}>{results.map((p) => card(p, "added"))}</Shelf>
62	        ) : viewAll ? (
63	          <Shelf title={SHELF_TITLES[viewAll]} back={() => setViewAll(null)} wrap>{shelves[viewAll].map((p) => card(p, viewAll))}</Shelf>
64	        ) : visible.length === 0 ? (
65	          <p className="mt-16 text-center text-base" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }}>{tag ? "No texts with this tag." : "Your library is empty. Use Add Text to add something to read."}</p>
66	        ) : (
67	          (["recent", "added", "progress"] as ShelfKey[]).map((k) =>
68	            shelves[k].length === 0 ? null : (
69	              <Shelf key={k} title={SHELF_TITLES[k]} onViewAll={k === "recent" ? undefined : () => setViewAll(k)}>
70	                {shelves[k].map((p) => card(p, k))}
71	              </Shelf>
72	            ),
73	          )
74	        )}
75	      </div>
76	
77	      {menu && (
78	        <CardMenu
79	          x={menu.x}
80	          y={menu.y}
81	          showHide={menu.shelf === "recent"}
82	          onClose={() => setMenu(null)}
83	          onPick={(kind) => {
84	            const p = menu.passage;
85	            setMenu(null);
86	            if (kind === "hide") {
87	              const pr = prog.get(p.id);
88	              saveProgress.mutate({ passage_id: p.id, word_index: pr?.word_index ?? 0, hidden_from_recent: true });
89	            } else setAction({ kind, passage: p });
90	          }}
91	        />
92	      )}
93	      {action?.kind === "view" && (
94	        <LocationDialog
95	          passage={action.passage}
96	          start={prog.get(action.passage.id)?.word_index ?? 0}
97	          onClose={() => setAction(null)}
98	          onPick={(i) => {
99	            saveProgress.mutate({ passage_id: action.passage.id, word_index: i, hidden_from_recent: false });
100	            navigate(rcReaderPath(action.passage.slug));
101	          }}
102	        />
103	      )}
104	      {action?.kind === "edit" && <AddTextScreen mode="edit" passage={action.passage} onClose={() => setAction(null)} onSaved={() => setAction(null)} />}
105	      {action?.kind === "image" && <EditImageDialog passage={action.passage} onClose={() => setAction(null)} />}
106	      {action?.kind === "tag" && <TagDialog passage={action.passage} allTags={[...new Set(passages.flatMap((p) => p.tags ?? []))]} onClose={() => setAction(null)} />}
107	      {action?.kind === "delete" && <DeleteDialog passage={action.passage} onClose={() => setAction(null)} />}
108	    </div>
109	  );
110	}
111	
112	function Shelf({ title, children, onViewAll, back, wrap = false }: { title: string; children: React.ReactNode; onViewAll?: () => void; back?: () => void; wrap?: boolean }): React.ReactElement {
113	  return (
114	    <section className="mb-9">
115	      <div className="mb-[13px] flex items-center gap-2">
116	        {back && <button type="button" aria-label="Back" onClick={back} className="flex"><MIcon name="arrow_back" /></button>}
117	        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold" style={{ color: "var(--rc-strong)" }}>{title}</h2>
118	        {onViewAll && (
119	          <button type="button" onClick={onViewAll} className="ml-3 flex items-center text-sm font-medium" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }}>
120	            View All <MIcon name="navigate_next" size={20} />
121	          </button>
122	        )}
123	      </div>
124	      <div className={`flex gap-3 ${wrap ? "flex-wrap" : "overflow-x-auto pb-2"}`}>{children}</div>
125	    </section>
126	  );
127	}
128	
129	function PassageCard({ passage, onMenu }: { passage: Passage; onMenu: (x: number, y: number) => void }): React.ReactElement {
130	  return (
131	    <div className="w-[148px] shrink-0">
132	      <Link to={rcReaderPath(passage.slug)} className="block h-[209px] w-[148px] overflow-hidden rounded" aria-label={passage.title}>
133	        {passage.cover_url ? (
134	          <img src={passage.cover_url} alt="" className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
135	        ) : (
136	          <div className="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center" style={{ background: "var(--rc-cover)" }}>
137	            <svg viewBox="0 0 72 92" width="72" height="92" aria-hidden>
138	              <path d="M8 0h40l24 24v60a8 8 0 0 1-8 8H8a8 8 0 0 1-8-8V8a8 8 0 0 1 8-8z" fill="#e6eef5" />
139	              <path d="M48 0v18a6 6 0 0 0 6 6h18z" fill="#c4d6e6" />
140	              <rect x="16" y="46" width="40" height="7" rx="1" fill="var(--rc-cover)" />
141	              <rect x="16" y="62" width="40" height="7" rx="1" fill="var(--rc-cover)" />
142	            </svg>
143	          </div>
144	        )}
145	      </Link>
146	      <div className="mt-[10px] flex items-start gap-1">
147	        <Link to={rcReaderPath(passage.slug)} className="line-clamp-2 flex-1 text-[13px] font-medium leading-4" style={{ color: "var(--rc-strong)" }}>{passage.title}</Link>
148	        <button
149	          type="button"
150	          aria-label={`More options for ${passage.title}`}
151	          onClick={(e) => {
152	            const r = (e.currentTarget as HTMLElement).getBoundingClientRect();
153	            onMenu(r.left, r.top);
154	          }}
155	          className="-mr-1 flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center"
156	          style={{ color: "var(--rc-strong)" }}
157	        >
158	          <MIcon name="more_vert" size={22} />
159	        </button>
160	      </div>
161	      {passage.author && <div className="mt-0.5 truncate text-[11px]" style={{ color: "#888" }}>{passage.author}</div>}
162	    </div>
163	  );
164	}
165	
166	type MenuKind = "view" | "image" | "edit" | "tag" | "delete" | "hide";
167	
168	function CardMenu({ x, y, showHide, onClose, onPick }: { x: number; y: number; showHide: boolean; onClose: () => void; onPick: (k: MenuKind) => void }): React.ReactElement {
169	  useEffect(() => {
170	    const close = (e: MouseEvent) => !(e.target as HTMLElement).closest("[data-rc-cardmenu]") && onClose();
171	    const esc = (e: KeyboardEvent) => e.key === "Escape" && onClose();
172	    window.addEventListener("mousedown", close);
173	    window.addEventListener("keydown", esc);
174	    return () => {
175	      window.removeEventListener("mousedown", close);
176	      window.removeEventListener("keydown", esc);
177	    };
178	  }, [onClose]);
179	  const items: [MenuKind, IconName, string][] = [
180	    ["view", "eye", "View/Location"],
181	    ["image", "image", "Edit Image"],
182	    ["edit", "create", "Edit Text"],
183	    ["tag", "pricetag", "Tag"],
184	    ["delete", "trash", "Delete"],
185	  ];
186	  if (showHide) items.push(["hide", "eyeOff", "Hide from Recent"]);
187	  const top = Math.max(8, Math.min(y - 300, window.innerHeight - 320));
188	  return createPortal(
189	    <div data-rc-cardmenu role="menu" className="rc-menu fixed z-50 w-[200px]" style={{ left: Math.max(8, x - 180), top }}>
190	      {items.map(([k, icon, label]) => (
191	        <button key={k} type="button" role="menuitem" className="rc-menu-item h-12" onClick={() => onPick(k)}>
192	          <Icon name={icon} size={16} /> {label}
193	        </button>
194	      ))}
195	    </div>,
196	    document.body,
197	  );
198	}
199	
200	/** View/Location: the View panel; picking a word opens the reader there. */
201	function LocationDialog({ passage, start, onClose, onPick }: { passage: Passage; start: number; onClose: () => void; onPick: (i: number) => void }): React.ReactElement {
202	  const words = useMemo(() => buildTextModel(passage.body).words, [passage.body]);
203	  return <ViewPanel open onClose={onClose} title={passage.title} words={words} currentWord={start} onJump={(w) => onPick(w.index)} onPlay={() => onPick(start)} />;
204	}
205	
206	function EditImageDialog({ passage, onClose }: { passage: Passage; onClose: () => void }): React.ReactElement {
207	  const upsert = useUpsertPassage();
208	  const [url, setUrl] = useState(passage.cover_url ?? "");
209	  const pickFile = (f: File) => {
210	    const r = new FileReader();
211	    r.onload = () => setUrl(String(r.result));
212	    r.readAsDataURL(f);
213	  };
214	  return (
215	    <Modal open onClose={onClose} width={480} closeOnEscape labelledBy="rc-img-title">
216	      <DialogHeader id="rc-img-title" title="Edit Image" onClose={onClose} />
217	      <div className="mt-4 flex gap-5 px-5">
218	        <div className="flex h-[150px] w-[106px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded" style={{ background: "var(--rc-cover)" }}>
219	          {url && <img src={url} alt="" className="h-full w-full object-cover" />}
220	        </div>
221	        <div className="flex-1 space-y-3 text-sm">
222	          <input value={url.startsWith("data:") ? "" : url} onChange={(e) => setUrl(e.target.value)} placeholder="Image URL" aria-label="Image URL" className="w-full bg-transparent pb-1 outline-none" style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #ccc", color: "var(--rc-strong)" }} />
223	          <label className="rc-outline-btn h-9 cursor-pointer">
224	            Upload image
225	            <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => e.target.files?.[0] && pickFile(e.target.files[0])} />
226	          </label>
227	          {url && <button type="button" className="block text-xs underline" onClick={() => setUrl("")}>Use the default cover</button>}
228	        </div>
229	      </div>
230	      <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end gap-1.5 px-5 pb-4">
231	        <button type="button" className="rc-outline-btn h-[38px]" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
232	        <button type="button" className="rc-green-btn" onClick={() => upsert.mutate({ id: passage.id, title: passage.title, body: passage.body, cover_url: url || null }, { onSuccess: onClose })}>Save</button>
233	      </div>
234	    </Modal>
235	  );
236	}
237	
238	function TagDialog({ passage, allTags, onClose }: { passage: Passage; allTags: string[]; onClose: () => void }): React.ReactElement {
239	  const upsert = useUpsertPassage();
240	  const [tags, setTags] = useState<string[]>(passage.tags ?? []);
241	  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
242	  const add = (t: string) => {
243	    const v = t.trim();
244	    if (v && !tags.includes(v)) setTags([...tags, v]);
245	    setInput("");
246	  };
247	  return (
248	    <Modal open onClose={onClose} width={480} closeOnEscape labelledBy="rc-tag-title">
249	      <DialogHeader id="rc-tag-title" title="Tag" onClose={onClose} />
250	      <div className="mt-4 px-5">
251	        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
252	          {tags.map((t) => (
253	            <span key={t} className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-2xl py-1 pl-3 pr-1.5 text-sm" style={{ background: "#e0e0e0", color: "rgba(0,0,0,.87)" }}>
254	              {t}
255	              <button type="button" aria-label={`Remove ${t}`} onClick={() => setTags(tags.filter((x) => x !== t))} className="flex"><MIcon name="cancel" size={18} style={{ color: "rgba(0,0,0,.4)" }} /></button>
256	            </span>
257	          ))}
258	        </div>
259	        <form onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); add(input); }}>
260	          <input value={input} onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)} list="rc-tags" placeholder="Add a tag and press Enter" aria-label="Add a tag" className="mt-4 w-full bg-transparent pb-1 text-sm outline-none" style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #ccc", color: "var(--rc-strong)" }} />
261	          <datalist id="rc-tags">{allTags.filter((t) => !tags.includes(t)).map((t) => <option key={t} value={t} />)}</datalist>
262	        </form>
263	      </div>
264	      <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end gap-1.5 px-5 pb-4">
265	        <button type="button" className="rc-outline-btn h-[38px]" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
266	        <button type="button" className="rc-green-btn" onClick={() => upsert.mutate({ id: passage.id, title: passage.title, body: passage.body, tags: input.trim() ? [...tags, input.trim()] : tags }, { onSuccess: onClose })}>Save</button>
267	      </div>
268	    </Modal>
269	  );
270	}
271	
272	function DeleteDialog({ passage, onClose }: { passage: Passage; onClose: () => void }): React.ReactElement {
273	  const del = useDeletePassage();
274	  return (
275	    <Modal open onClose={onClose} width={420} closeOnEscape labelledBy="rc-del-title">
276	      <DialogHeader id="rc-del-title" title="Delete" onClose={onClose} />
277	      <p className="mt-3 px-5 text-sm" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }}>
278	        Delete "{passage.title}"? Its reading progress and bookmarks are removed too. This cannot be undone.
279	      </p>
280	      <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end gap-1.5 px-5 pb-4">
281	        <button type="button" className="rc-outline-btn h-[38px]" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
282	        <button type="button" className="inline-flex h-[38px] items-center rounded bg-[#f44336] px-4 text-sm font-medium text-white" onClick={() => del.mutate(passage.id, { onSuccess: onClose })}>Delete</button>
283	      </div>
284	    </Modal>
285	  );
286	}

"""jsfind.py <file> <literal> [before] [after] [max]: print context around each literal hit, ';' -> newline."""
import sys

path, needle = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
before = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 300
after = int(sys.argv[4]) if len(sys.argv) > 4 else 300
limit = int(sys.argv[5]) if len(sys.argv) > 5 else 5
s = open(path, encoding="utf-8").read()
i, n = 0, 0
while n < limit:
    i = s.find(needle, i)
    if i < 0:
        break
    print(f"===== @{i}")
    print(s[max(0, i - before): i + after].replace(";", ";\n"))
    i += len(needle)
    n += 1

import React from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { usePassages } from "../hooks/useReadingCenterData";
import { rcTagPath } from "../lib/routes";
import { Icon } from "../components/Icon";
import { TopBar } from "../components/AppShell";

/** Tags: every tag used on a text; opening one filters the library (Spreeder library/tags/:name). */
export default function TagsPage(): React.ReactElement {
  const passages = usePassages().data ?? [];
  const counts = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const p of passages) for (const t of p.tags ?? []) counts.set(t, (counts.get(t) ?? 0) + 1);
  const tags = [...counts.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[0].localeCompare(b[0]));
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen">
      <TopBar />
      <div className="px-5">
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-bold" style={{ color: "var(--rc-strong)" }}>Tags</h2>
        {tags.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="mt-16 text-center text-base" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }}>No tags yet. Use Tag on a text's ⋮ menu to add one.</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
            {tags.map(([t, n]) => (
              <Link key={t} to={rcTagPath(t)} className="flex h-12 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-4 text-sm font-medium" style={{ background: "var(--rc-card)", color: "var(--rc-strong)" }}>
                <Icon name="pricetag" size={16} /> {t} <span style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }}>({n})</span>
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
import { SEED_PASSAGE } from "../data/seed";
import { DEFAULT_PREFERENCES } from "./constants";
import { countWords } from "./tokenize";
import type { Bookmark, Passage, Preferences, Progress, ReadingSession } from "./types";

/**
 * Browser storage for the standalone app: one JSON document in localStorage.
 * It plays the part of the five reading_center_* tables. On integration into
 * LSAT U, hooks/useReadingCenterData.ts goes back to Supabase and this file
 * is deleted; nothing else reads it except the passage-manager delete check.
 */
interface Db {
  version: 1;
  passages: Passage[];
  preferences: Preferences;
  progress: Progress[];
  bookmarks: Bookmark[];
  sessions: ReadingSession[];
  /** Shown as "<name>'s preferences" and "<name>'s Spreeder"-style headings. */
  profileName?: string;
}

const KEY = "reading-center:v1";

function seedDb(): Db {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  return {
    version: 1,
    passages: [
      {
        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
        slug: SEED_PASSAGE.slug,
        title: SEED_PASSAGE.title,
        author: null,
        body: SEED_PASSAGE.body,
        cover_url: null,
        word_count: countWords(SEED_PASSAGE.body),
        sort_order: 1,
        is_published: true,
        created_at: now,
        updated_at: now,
      },
    ],
    preferences: DEFAULT_PREFERENCES,
    progress: [],
    bookmarks: [],
    sessions: [],
  };
}

let memory: Db | null = null;

export function readDb(): Db {
  if (memory) return memory;
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
    if (raw) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Db;
      memory = { ...seedDb(), ...parsed, preferences: { ...DEFAULT_PREFERENCES, ...parsed.preferences } };
      return memory;
    }
  } catch {
    // Blocked or corrupt storage: fall through to a fresh in-memory copy.
  }
  memory = seedDb();
  writeDb(memory);
  return memory;
}

export function writeDb(next: Db): void {
  memory = next;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(next));
  } catch {
    // Storage full or blocked: keep working from memory for this tab.
  }
}

export function updateDb(fn: (db: Db) => Db): Db {
  const next = fn(readDb());

312	          <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 bottom-[50px] flex items-center justify-center gap-[50px]">
313	            <TransportButton label="Previous" onClick={engine.prev}>
314	              <svg viewBox="0 0 50 50" width="50" height="50" aria-hidden><path d="M33 13 L15 25 L33 37 Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="4" strokeLinejoin="round" /></svg>
315	            </TransportButton>
316	            <TransportButton label="Play" onClick={engine.play} big>
317	              <svg viewBox="0 0 60 60" width="60" height="60" aria-hidden><circle cx="30" cy="30" r="27" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="5" /><path d="M24 18 L42 30 L24 42 Z" fill="currentColor" /></svg>
318	            </TransportButton>
319	            <TransportButton label="Next" onClick={engine.next}>
320	              <svg viewBox="0 0 50 50" width="50" height="50" aria-hidden><path d="M17 13 L35 25 L17 37 Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="4" strokeLinejoin="round" /></svg>
321	            </TransportButton>
322	          </div>
323	        )}
324	      </main>
325	
326	      {/* Bottom bar */}
327	      {isEReader ? (
328	        <EReaderBar page={page} pageCount={pageCount} onPage={goPage} />
329	      ) : (
330	        <footer className="grid h-10 shrink-0 grid-cols-3 items-center px-5" style={{ background: "var(--rc-bottombar)", borderTop: "0.8px solid var(--rc-bottombar-border)" }}>
331	          <div className="flex justify-center">
332	            <button ref={kbdBtn} type="button" aria-label="Keyboard shortcuts" onClick={() => setKbdOpen((o) => !o)} className="flex h-8 w-10 items-center justify-center" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text)" }}>
333	              <MIcon name="keyboard" size={24} />
334	            </button>
335	          </div>
336	          <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-[19px]">
337	            <BarButton onClick={() => { engine.pause(); setWpmOpen(true); }} icon="speedometer">{prefs.wpm}</BarButton>
338	            <BarButton onClick={() => { engine.pause(); setViewOpen(true); }} icon="list">View</BarButton>
339	          </div>
340	          <div className="pl-[100px] text-sm leading-[31px]" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text)", opacity: 0.8 }}>
341	            {pct}% - {formatDuration(remaining)} remaining
342	          </div>
343	        </footer>
344	      )}
345	
346	      <ShortcutsPopover open={kbdOpen} anchorX={kbdBtn.current?.getBoundingClientRect().left ?? 230} onClose={() => setKbdOpen(false)} />
347	      <PreferencesDialog open={prefsOpen} onClose={() => setPrefsOpen(false)} prefs={prefs} onChange={updatePrefs} name={name} />
348	      <WpmDialog open={wpmOpen} onClose={() => setWpmOpen(false)} value={prefs.wpm} onSave={changeSpeed} />
349	      <ViewPanel
350	        open={viewOpen}
351	        onClose={() => setViewOpen(false)}
352	        title={passage.title}
353	        words={model.words}
354	        currentWord={activeWord}
355	        footer={`${formatDuration(remaining)} to go - ${prefs.wpm} WPM`}
356	        onJump={(w) => engine.goToWord(w.index)}
357	        onPlay={engine.play}
358	      />
359	      <BookmarksDialog
360	        open={bmOpen}
361	        onClose={() => setBmOpen(false)}
362	        bookmarks={bookmarks.data ?? []}
363	        words={model.words}
364	        onOpenBookmark={(b: Bookmark) => { setBmOpen(false); setNote({ mode: "edit", word: model.words[b.word_index], bookmark: b }); }}
365	      />
366	      <WordActionSheet word={sheetWord} onClose={() => setSheetWord(null)} onBookmark={openBookmarkFor} />
367	      <BookmarkNoteDialog
368	        state={note}
369	        title={passage.title}
370	        onClose={() => setNote(null)}
371	        onSave={(text) => {
372	          if (note?.mode === "edit") updateBookmark.mutate({ id: note.bookmark.id, note: text || null });
373	          else if (note) addBookmark.mutate({ passage_id: passage.id, word_index: note.word.index, note: text || null });
374	          setNote(null);
375	        }}
376	        onDelete={(b) => { deleteBookmark.mutate({ id: b.id, passage_id: passage.id }); setNote(null); }}
377	        onGoTo={(w) => { engine.goToWord(w.index); if (isEReader) setPage(pageOf(w.index)); setNote(null); }}
378	      />
379	      {isEReader && (
380	        <EReaderSearch open={searchOpen} onClose={() => { setSearchOpen(false); setMarks([]); }} words={model.words} pageOf={pageOf} onSelect={(p, hits) => { setPage(p); setMarks(hits); }} />
381	      )}
382	      <FinishAlert
383	        open={finished}
384	        title={passage.title}
385	        onOkay={() => {
386	          saveProgress.mutate({ passage_id: passage.id, word_index: 0, completed: true });
387	          if (document.fullscreenElement) document.exitFullscreen();
388	          navigate(RC_LIBRARY_PATH);
389	        }}
390	      />
391	    </div>
392	  );
393	}

164	// ---------------------------------------------------------------- bookmarks
165	
166	export function BookmarksDialog({
167	  open,
168	  onClose,
169	  bookmarks,
170	  words,
171	  onOpenBookmark,
172	}: {
173	  open: boolean;
174	  onClose: () => void;
175	  bookmarks: Bookmark[];
176	  words: Word[];
177	  onOpenBookmark: (b: Bookmark) => void;
178	}): React.ReactElement {
179	  const sorted = [...bookmarks].sort((a, b) => a.word_index - b.word_index);
180	  return (
181	    <Modal open={open} onClose={onClose} width={850} height={480} closeOnEscape labelledBy="rc-bm-title">
182	      <DialogHeader id="rc-bm-title" title="Bookmarks" onClose={onClose} />
183	      {sorted.length === 0 ? (
184	        <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center pb-8 text-center">
185	          <div className="flex h-[150px] w-[150px] items-center justify-center rounded-full" style={{ background: "#757575" }}>
186	            <Icon name="list" size={84} style={{ color: "#fff" }} />
187	          </div>
188	          <div className="mt-4 text-[28px]" style={{ color: "#616161" }}>No Bookmarks Yet</div>
189	          <p className="mt-3 max-w-[760px] text-base font-medium" style={{ color: "#757575" }}>
190	            To create a bookmark, simply pause Spreeder while you're reading and long press or click on any word.
191	          </p>
192	        </div>
193	      ) : (
194	        <ul className="mt-4 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-5">
195	          {sorted.map((b) => (
196	            <li key={b.id} className="border-b" style={{ borderColor: "#cccccc" }}>
197	              <button type="button" onClick={() => onOpenBookmark(b)} className="flex w-full items-center gap-4 py-3 text-left text-sm hover:bg-[var(--rc-menu-hover)]">
198	                <span className="w-[30%] truncate font-medium">{words[b.word_index]?.text.replace(/[^\w\s'-]/g, "")}</span>
199	                <span className="w-[50%] truncate" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }}>{b.note || ""}</span>
200	                <span className="w-[20%] text-right" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }}>{new Date(b.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
201	              </button>
202	            </li>
203	          ))}
204	        </ul>
205	      )}
206	    </Modal>
207	  );
208	}
209	
210	export type BookmarkNoteState = { mode: "create"; word: Word } | { mode: "edit"; word: Word; bookmark: Bookmark };
211	
212	/** Spreeder view-bookmark: chips (title, Position, word), optional note, Go to location. */
213	export function BookmarkNoteDialog({
214	  state,
215	  title,
216	  onClose,
217	  onSave,
218	  onDelete,
219	  onGoTo,
220	}: {
221	  state: BookmarkNoteState | null;
222	  title: string;
223	  onClose: () => void;
224	  onSave: (note: string) => void;
225	  onDelete: (b: Bookmark) => void;
226	  onGoTo: (w: Word) => void;
227	}): React.ReactElement {
228	  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
229	  useEffect(() => {
230	    setNote(state?.mode === "edit" ? state.bookmark.note ?? "" : "");
231	  }, [state]);
232	  const word = state?.word;
233	  const clean = word ? word.text.replace(/[^\w\s'-]/g, "").replace(/'s$/i, "").trim() : "";
234	  const chip = (t: string) => (
235	    <span className="max-w-[320px] truncate rounded-2xl px-3 py-1.5 text-sm" style={{ background: "#e0e0e0", color: "rgba(0,0,0,.87)" }}>{t}</span>
236	  );
237	  return (
238	    <Modal open={!!state} onClose={onClose} width={600} height={430} closeOnEscape labelledBy="rc-note-title">
239	      <DialogHeader id="rc-note-title" title={state?.mode === "edit" ? "Edit Bookmark/Note" : "Create Bookmark/Note"} onClose={onClose} />
240	      <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2 px-5">
241	        {chip(title)}
242	        {word && chip(`Position ${word.sectionIndex + 1}`)}
243	        {chip(clean)}
244	      </div>
245	      <div className="mt-3 flex-1 px-5">
246	        <textarea
247	          aria-label="Enter Notes (Optional)..."
248	          placeholder="Enter Notes (Optional)..."
249	          value={note}
250	          onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)}
251	          className="mt-4 h-[150px] w-full resize-none p-[5px] text-sm outline-none"
252	          style={{ border: "1px solid #ccc", background: "transparent", color: "var(--rc-text)" }}
253	        />
254	      </div>
255	      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5 px-5 pb-5">
256	        {state?.mode === "edit" && (
257	          <>
258	            <button type="button" className="rc-outline-btn mr-auto h-[38px]" onClick={() => onDelete(state.bookmark)}>Delete</button>
259	            <button type="button" className="rc-outline-btn h-[38px]" onClick={() => word && onGoTo(word)}>Go to location</button>
260	          </>
261	        )}
262	        <button type="button" className="rc-outline-btn h-[38px]" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
263	        <button type="button" className="rc-green-btn" onClick={() => onSave(note)}>Save</button>
264	      </div>
265	    </Modal>
266	  );
267	}

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";
import type { Bookmark, Preferences, ReaderTheme, ReadingMode } from "../lib/types";
import type { Word } from "../lib/spreeder";
import { DEFAULT_PREFERENCES, FONTS, FONT_SIZES, READING_MODES, THEMES } from "../lib/constants";
import { clampWpm } from "../lib/spreeder";
import { DialogHeader, Dropdown, HelpTip, Modal, PillTabs, TickSlider, Toggle } from "./spreeder-ui";
import { Icon, MIcon } from "./Icon";

const fontStackOf = (label: string): string => FONTS.find((f) => f.label === label)?.stack ?? FONTS[0].stack;

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- preferences

type PrefTab = "spreed" | "appearance" | "advanced";

}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- finish + toast

export function FinishAlert({ title, open, onOkay }: { title: string; open: boolean; onOkay: () => void }): React.ReactElement | null {
  if (!open) return null;
  return createPortal(
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center" style={{ background: "rgba(0,0,0,.32)" }}>
      <div role="alertdialog" aria-labelledby="rc-finish-h" className="w-[280px] rounded p-5 pb-2" style={{ background: "var(--rc-dialog)", color: "var(--rc-text)", boxShadow: "0 11px 15px -7px rgba(0,0,0,.2), 0 24px 38px 3px rgba(0,0,0,.14)" }}>
        <h2 id="rc-finish-h" className="text-base font-medium" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }}>Congratulations!</h2>
        <p className="mt-3 text-sm" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }}>
          You have successfully completed {title}. It will now be moved to your finished section on the Home Screen.
        </p>
        <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end">
          <button type="button" className="h-9 rounded px-3 text-sm font-medium uppercase" style={{ color: "#3880ff" }} onClick={onOkay}>Okay</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body,
  );
}

/** "N WPM" pill: fades in .5s, holds 2s, fades out 2s (Spreeder updateNotificationText). */
export function SpeedToast({ text, nonce }: { text: string; nonce: number }): React.ReactElement {
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!nonce) return;
    setVisible(true);
    const t = window.setTimeout(() => setVisible(false), 2000);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
  }, [nonce]);
  return (
    <div
      aria-live="polite"
      className="pointer-events-none absolute left-1/2 top-[10px] z-[4] -translate-x-1/2 whitespace-nowrap rounded px-2 py-2 text-sm text-white"
      style={{ background: "#323232", opacity: visible ? 1 : 0, transition: visible ? "opacity .5s" : "opacity 2s" }}
    >
      {text}
    </div>
  );
}

    --rc-tab-idle: #e0e0e0;

  html, body {
    background: var(--rc-page);

    font-family: Poppins, sans-serif;
  }
  * { border-color: var(--rc-outline); }

@layer components {
  .rc-outline-btn {
    @apply inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded px-4 text-sm font-medium transition;
    height: 36px;
    border: 0.8px solid var(--rc-outline);
    color: var(--rc-strong);
  }
  .rc-outline-btn:hover { background: var(--rc-menu-hover); }
  .rc-green-btn {
    @apply inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded px-4 text-sm font-medium text-white transition;
    height: 38px;
    background: var(--rc-green);
    border: 0.8px solid var(--rc-green);
  }
  .rc-green-btn:disabled { opacity: 0.4; cursor: default; }
  .rc-menu {
    @apply rounded py-2 text-[15px];
    background: var(--rc-menu);
    color: var(--rc-text);
    box-shadow: 0 2px 4px -1px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2), 0 4px 5px 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.14), 0 1px 10px 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12);
  }
  .rc-menu-item {
    @apply flex w-full items-center gap-3 px-4 text-left;
    height: 48px;
  }
  .rc-menu-item:hover, .rc-menu-item[data-active="true"] { background: var(--rc-menu-hover); }
}

14:export function progressCountForPassage(passageId: string): number {
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient, type UseQueryResult, type UseMutationResult } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { readDb, updateDb } from "../lib/store";
import { countWords, slugify } from "../lib/tokenize";
import type { Passage, Preferences, Progress, Bookmark, ReadingSession } from "../lib/types";

/**
 * Same hook names and shapes as the LSAT U (Supabase) version, backed by
 * browser storage. Integration swaps this one file back.
 */

const byOrder = (a: Passage, b: Passage): number => a.sort_order - b.sort_order || a.title.localeCompare(b.title);

export function usePassages(): UseQueryResult<Passage[], Error> {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["reading-center", "passages"],
    queryFn: async (): Promise<Passage[]> => [...readDb().passages].sort(byOrder),
  });
}

export function usePassageBySlug(slug: string | undefined): UseQueryResult<Passage | null, Error> {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["reading-center", "passage", slug],
    queryFn: async (): Promise<Passage | null> => readDb().passages.find((p) => p.slug === slug) ?? null,
    enabled: !!slug,
  });
}

export function usePreferences(): UseQueryResult<Preferences, Error> {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["reading-center", "prefs"],
      updateDb((db) => ({ ...db, sessions: [...db.sessions, { id: crypto.randomUUID(), ...session }] }));
    },
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["reading-center", "sessions"] }),
  });
}

export function useUpsertPassage(): UseMutationResult<
  Passage,
  Error,
  Partial<Passage> & { title: string; body: string }
> {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: Partial<Passage> & { title: string; body: string }): Promise<Passage> => {
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      let saved: Passage | null = null;
      updateDb((db) => {
        const existing = input.id ? db.passages.find((p) => p.id === input.id) : undefined;
        const slug = existing?.slug ?? input.slug ?? slugify(input.title);
        if (!existing && db.passages.some((p) => p.slug === slug)) {
          throw new Error(`A passage with the address "${slug}" already exists. Change the title.`);
        }
        saved = {
          id: existing?.id ?? crypto.randomUUID(),
          author: null,
          cover_url: null,
          is_published: true,
          sort_order: db.passages.reduce((m, p) => Math.max(m, p.sort_order), 0) + 1,
          created_at: now,
          ...existing,
          ...input,
          slug,
          word_count: countWords(input.body),
          updated_at: now,
        } as Passage;
        return { ...db, passages: [...db.passages.filter((p) => p.id !== saved!.id), saved] };
      });
      return saved!;
    },
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["reading-center"] }),
  });
}

export function useDeletePassage(): UseMutationResult<void, Error, string> {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
      // Mirrors the on-delete-cascade in the LSAT U migration.
      updateDb((db) => ({
        ...db,
        passages: db.passages.filter((p) => p.id !== id),
        progress: db.progress.filter((p) => p.passage_id !== id),
        bookmarks: db.bookmarks.filter((b) => b.passage_id !== id),
        sessions: db.sessions.filter((s) => s.passage_id !== id),
      }));
    },
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["reading-center"] }),
  });
}

/**
 * Standalone app paths, mirroring Spreeder (/library, /spreed, /profile, ...).
 * On integration into LSAT U these become the LSAT U paths; every page links
 * through these constants, so this is the only file that changes.
 */
export const RC_LIBRARY_PATH = "/";
export const RC_TAGS_PATH = "/tags";
export const RC_COURSES_PATH = "/courses";
export const RC_PROFILE_PATH = "/profile";
export const RC_STATS_PATH = "/profile";
export const RC_MANAGE_PATH = "/manage";

export function rcReaderPath(slug: string): string {
  return `/read/${slug}`;
}

export function rcTagPath(tag: string): string {
  return `/tags/${encodeURIComponent(tag)}`;
}
import { BrowserRouter, Route, Routes } from "react-router-dom";
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Toaster } from "sonner";
import { AppShell, useAppTheme } from "@/features/reading-center/components/AppShell";
import LibraryPage from "@/features/reading-center/pages/LibraryPage";
import ReaderPage from "@/features/reading-center/pages/ReaderPage";
import ProfilePage from "@/features/reading-center/pages/ProfilePage";
import TagsPage from "@/features/reading-center/pages/TagsPage";
import CoursesPage from "@/features/reading-center/pages/CoursesPage";

const queryClient = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false, refetchOnWindowFocus: false } } });

function ThemedRoutes() {
  useAppTheme();
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route element={<AppShell />}>
        <Route path="/" element={<LibraryPage />} />
        <Route path="/tags" element={<TagsPage />} />
        <Route path="/tags/:tag" element={<LibraryPage />} />
        <Route path="/courses" element={<CoursesPage />} />
        <Route path="/profile" element={<ProfilePage />} />
      </Route>
      <Route path="/read/:slug" element={<ReaderPage />} />
      <Route path="*" element={<LibraryPage />} />
    </Routes>
  );
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <BrowserRouter>
        <ThemedRoutes />
      </BrowserRouter>
      <Toaster position="bottom-center" />
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
}
export type ReadingMode = "serial" | "flow" | "highlight" | "scroll" | "ereader";
export type ReaderTheme = "light" | "dark" | "high_contrast" | "system";

export interface Passage {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  author: string | null;
  body: string;
  cover_url: string | null;
  word_count: number;
  sort_order: number;
  is_published: boolean;
  /** Spreeder tags (standalone only for now). */
  tags?: string[];
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

export interface Preferences {
  wpm: number;
  reading_mode: ReadingMode;
  number_of_lines: number;
  words_per_line: number;
  center_text: boolean;
  theme: ReaderTheme;
  font_family: string;
  font_size: number;
  line_spacing: number;
  punctuation_pauses: boolean;
  speed_variability: boolean;
  goal_wpm: number;
}

export interface Progress {
  passage_id: string;
  word_index: number;
  completed_at: string | null;
  last_opened_at: string;
  hidden_from_recent: boolean;
  const next = fn(readDb());
  writeDb(next);
  return next;
}

export function profileName(): string {
  return readDb().profileName || "Reader";
}

export function setProfileName(name: string): void {
  updateDb((db) => ({ ...db, profileName: name.trim() || "Reader" }));
}

export function progressCountForPassage(passageId: string): number {
  return readDb().progress.filter((p) => p.passage_id === passageId && p.word_index > 0).length;
}

[])];\n      for (const p of db.passages) for (const t of p.tags ?? []) if (!list.some((x) => x.name === t)) list.push({ name: t, color: 0 });\n      return list;\n    },\n  });\n}\n\n/** Create a tag, or rename / recolour one (oldName set). Renames carry through to passages. */\nexport function useSaveTag(): UseMutationResult<UserTag, Error, { oldName?: string; name: string; color?: number }> {\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n  return useMutation({\n    mutationFn: async ({ oldName, name, color }): Promise<UserTag> => {\n      const clean = name.trim();\n      if (!clean) throw new Error(\"Enter a tag name.\");\n      let saved: UserTag = { name: clean, color: color ?? 0 };\n      updateDb((db) => {\n        const tags = [...(db.tags ?? [])];\n        for (const p of db.passages) for (const t of p.tags ?? []) if (!tags.some((x) => x.name === t)) tags.push({ name: t, color: 0 });\n        const i = oldName != null ? tags.findIndex((t) => t.name === oldName) : -1;\n        if (tags.some((t, j) => t.name === clean && j !== i)) throw new Error(`A tag named \"${clean}\" already exists.`);\n        if (i >= 0) {\n          saved = { name: clean, color: color ?? tags[i].color };\n          tags[i] = saved;\n        } else tags.push(saved);\n        const passages = oldName != null && oldName !== clean\n          ? db.passages.map((p) => (p.tags?.includes(oldName) ? { ...p, tags: p.tags.map((t) => (t === oldName ? clean : t)) } : p))\n          : db.passages;\n        return { ...db, tags, passages };\n      });\n      return saved;\n    },\n    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"reading-center\"] }),\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useDeleteTag(): UseMutationResult<void, Error, string> {\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n  return useMutation({\n    mutationFn: async (name: string): Promise<void> => {\n      updateDb((db) => ({\n        ...db,\n        tags: (db.tags ?? []).filter((t) => t.name !== name),\n        passages: db.passages.map((p) => (p.tags?.includes(name) ? { ...p, tags: p.tags.filter((t) => t !== name) } : p)),\n      }));\n    },\n    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"reading-center\"] }),\n  });\n}\n'''\nopen(p,'w',encoding='utf-8').write(s)\nprint('ok')\nEOF", "description": "Add user tags to types, store and hooks"}

12	export function Modal({
13	  open,
14	  onClose,
15	  width,
16	  height,
17	  children,
18	  closeOnBackdrop = false,
19	  closeOnEscape = false,
20	  fullScreen = false,
21	  labelledBy,
22	}: {
23	  open: boolean;
24	  onClose: () => void;
25	  width?: number | string;
26	  height?: number | string;
27	  children: React.ReactNode;
28	  closeOnBackdrop?: boolean;
29	  closeOnEscape?: boolean;
30	  fullScreen?: boolean;
31	  labelledBy?: string;
32	}): React.ReactElement | null {
33	  useEffect(() => {
34	    if (!open || !closeOnEscape) return;
35	    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => e.key === "Escape" && onClose();
36	    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
37	    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
38	  }, [open, closeOnEscape, onClose]);
39	  if (!open) return null;
40	  return createPortal(
41	    <div
42	      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center"
43	      style={{ background: fullScreen ? "transparent" : "var(--rc-backdrop)" }}
44	      onMouseDown={(e) => closeOnBackdrop && e.target === e.currentTarget && onClose()}
45	    >
46	      <div
47	        role="dialog"
48	        aria-modal="true"
49	        aria-labelledby={labelledBy}
50	        data-rc-dialog
51	        className="relative flex flex-col"
52	        style={
53	          fullScreen
54	            ? { position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "var(--rc-dialog)", color: "var(--rc-text)" }
55	            : {
56	                width,
57	                height,
58	                maxWidth: "calc(100vw - 32px)",
59	                maxHeight: "calc(100vh - 32px)",
60	                background: "var(--rc-dialog)",
61	                color: "var(--rc-text)",
62	                borderRadius: 15,
63	                border: "0.8px solid var(--rc-dialog-border)",
64	                boxShadow: "var(--rc-dialog-shadow)",
65	                padding: 14,
66	              }
67	        }
68	      >
69	        {children}
70	      </div>
71	    </div>,
72	    document.body,
73	  );
74	}
75	
76	export function DialogHeader({ title, onClose, id }: { title: React.ReactNode; onClose: () => void; id?: string }): React.ReactElement {
77	  return (
78	    <div className="flex items-start justify-between px-5 pt-1.5">
79	      <h3 id={id} className="text-2xl font-normal leading-8" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text)" }}>
80	        {title}
81	      </h3>
82	      <button type="button" aria-label="Close" onClick={onClose} className="mt-1 flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full hover:bg-[var(--rc-menu-hover)]" style={{ color: "var(--rc-strong)" }}>
83	        <MIcon name="close" />
84	      </button>
85	    </div>
86	  );
87	}

## user

Tool result:
1	import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
2	import { useUpsertPassage } from "../hooks/useReadingCenterData";
3	import type { Passage } from "../lib/types";
4	import { Modal } from "./spreeder-ui";
5	import { MIcon } from "./Icon";
6	
7	/**
8	 * Spreeder "Add To Library": full-screen title + text, Cancel / Save.
9	 * Paste Text opens it empty; Import Document reads a local text file into it;
10	 * Edit Text (card menu) opens it filled.
11	 */
12	export function AddTextScreen({
13	  mode,
14	  passage,
15	  onClose,
16	  onSaved,
17	}: {
18	  mode: "paste" | "import" | "edit";
19	  passage?: Passage;
20	  onClose: () => void;
21	  onSaved: (slug: string) => void;
22	}): React.ReactElement {
23	  const upsert = useUpsertPassage();
24	  const [title, setTitle] = useState(passage?.title ?? "");
25	  const [body, setBody] = useState(passage?.body ?? "");
26	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
27	  const file = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
28	
29	  useEffect(() => {
30	    if (mode === "import") file.current?.click();
31	  }, [mode]);
32	
33	  const readFile = async (f: File) => {
34	    const text = await f.text();
35	    const plain = /\.html?$/i.test(f.name) ? new DOMParser().parseFromString(text, "text/html").body.innerText : text;
36	    setTitle((t) => t || f.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, ""));
37	    setBody(plain.trim());
38	  };
39	
40	  const canSave = title.trim() !== "" && body.trim() !== "" && !upsert.isPending;
41	  const save = () =>
42	    upsert.mutate(
43	      passage ? { id: passage.id, title: title.trim(), body: body.trim() } : { title: title.trim(), body: body.trim() },
44	      { onSuccess: (p) => onSaved(p.slug), onError: (e) => setError(e.message) },
45	    );
46	
47	  return (
48	    <Modal open onClose={onClose} fullScreen closeOnEscape labelledBy="rc-add-title">
49	      <div className="flex h-full flex-col px-[30px] pb-5 pt-4">
50	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
51	          <h2 id="rc-add-title" className="pl-1 text-2xl" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text)" }}>{mode === "edit" ? "Edit Text" : "Add To Library"}</h2>
52	          <button type="button" aria-label="Close" onClick={onClose} style={{ color: "var(--rc-strong)" }}><MIcon name="close" /></button>
53	        </div>
54	        <input
55	          value={title}
56	          onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
57	          placeholder="Enter Title"
58	          aria-label="Enter Title"
59	          className="mt-[38px] w-full bg-transparent pb-3 text-[13px] font-medium outline-none placeholder:text-[var(--rc-text)]"
60	          style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid rgba(128,128,128,.5)", color: "var(--rc-text)" }}
61	        />
62	        <textarea
63	          value={body}
64	          onChange={(e) => setBody(e.target.value)}
65	          placeholder="Add Text Here"
66	          aria-label="Add Text Here"
67	          className="mt-[42px] min-h-0 w-full flex-1 resize-none rounded-sm bg-transparent p-4 text-[13px] font-medium outline-none placeholder:text-[var(--rc-text)]"
68	          style={{ border: "1px solid rgba(128,128,128,.6)", color: "var(--rc-text)" }}
69	        />
70	        {error && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-500">{error}</p>}
71	        <div className="mt-8 flex items-center justify-end gap-6 pr-6">
72	          {mode !== "edit" && (
73	            <>
74	              <input ref={file} type="file" accept=".txt,.md,.text,.html,.htm,text/plain,text/markdown,text/html" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => e.target.files?.[0] && void readFile(e.target.files[0])} />
75	              <button type="button" className="mr-auto text-[13px] font-medium underline-offset-2 hover:underline" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }} onClick={() => file.current?.click()}>
76	                Import Document
77	              </button>
78	            </>
79	          )}
80	          <button type="button" className="rc-outline-btn h-[38px]" style={{ borderColor: "rgba(128,128,128,.6)" }} onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
81	          <button type="button" className="h-[38px] px-4 text-sm font-medium" disabled={!canSave} onClick={save} style={{ color: canSave ? "var(--rc-green)" : "rgba(128,128,128,.6)" }}>
82	            Save
83	          </button>
84	        </div>
85	      </div>
86	    </Modal>
87	  );
88	}
89

171	export function highlightPages(lines: Line[], stageHeight: number, fontSize: number, numberOfLines: number): Page[] {
172	  const linesPerPage = Math.max(1, Math.floor((stageHeight - 20) / fontSize));
173	  const groupsPerPage = Math.max(1, Math.floor(linesPerPage / numberOfLines));
174	  const perPage = groupsPerPage * numberOfLines;
175	  const pages: Page[] = [];
176	  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += perPage) {
177	    const pl = lines.slice(i, i + perPage);
178	    const groups: Chunk[] = [];
179	    for (let g = 0; g < pl.length; g += numberOfLines) groups.push(toChunk(pl.slice(g, g + numberOfLines)));
180	    pages.push({ lines: pl, groups });
181	  }
182	  return pages;
183	}
184	
185	// ---------------------------------------------------------------- timing
186	
187	const PUNCTUATION = /[.?!:;\-–,]/;
188	
189	export interface DelayOptions {
190	  wpm: number;
191	  numberOfLines: number;
192	  speedVariability: boolean;
193	  punctuationPauses: boolean;
194	}
195	
196	/** Spreeder computeSwitchingTimeDelays for one section. */
197	export function chunkDelays(section: Section, chunks: Chunk[], opts: DelayOptions): number[] {
198	  const n = chunks.length;
199	  if (n === 0) return [];
200	  const total = Math.round((section.words.length / opts.wpm) * 60000);
201	  const perChunk = Math.round(total / n);
202	  const avgChars = section.text.length / n;
203	  const lastLines = chunks[n - 1].lines.length;
204	  const even = evenChunkDelays(total, n, lastLines, opts.numberOfLines);
205	  return chunks.map((c, i) => {
206	    const notLast = i + 1 < n;
207	    let d = opts.speedVariability ? perChunk * ((c.text.length + (notLast ? 1 : 0)) / avgChars) : notLast ? even.even : even.last;
208	    if (opts.punctuationPauses && PUNCTUATION.test(c.text)) d *= 2;
209	    return d;
210	  });
211	}
212	
213	export function evenChunkDelays(total: number, count: number, lastLines: number, numberOfLines: number): { even: number; last: number } {
214	  if (count <= 1) return { even: total, last: total };
215	  const f = Math.min(Math.max(lastLines / numberOfLines, 0), 1);
216	  const even = total / (count - 1 + f);
217	  return { even, last: even * f };
218	}

/** Spreeder computeTotalTimeBySection: word-count chunks of groupSize * lines, used for "remaining". */
export function sectionTime(text: string, opts: { wpm: number; groupSize: number; numberOfLines: number; punctuationPauses: boolean }): number {
  if (!text) return 0;
  const n = Math.max(1, opts.groupSize * opts.numberOfLines);
  const chunks: string[] = Array.from(text.match(new RegExp(`[\\S]+(?:\\s+[\\S]+){0,${n - 1}}`, "g")) ?? []);
  const totalWords = text.split(" ").length;
  const total = Math.round((totalWords / opts.wpm) * 60000);
  if (!opts.punctuationPauses) return total;
  const per = Math.round(total / chunks.length);
  return chunks.reduce((sum, c) => sum + (PUNCTUATION.test(c) ? 2 * per : per), 0);
}

export function remainingMs(model: TextModel, wordIndex: number, opts: { wpm: number; groupSize: number; numberOfLines: number; punctuationPauses: boolean }): number {
  const word = model.words[wordIndex] ?? model.words[0];
  if (!word) return 0;
  const section = model.sections[word.sectionIndex];
  const offset = word.index - section.words[0].index;
  const rest = section.words.slice(offset).map((w) => w.text).join(" ");
  let ms = sectionTime(rest, opts);
  for (let s = word.sectionIndex + 1; s < model.sections.length; s++) ms += sectionTime(model.sections[s].text, opts);
  return ms;
}

## assistant

You've hit your usage limit, so I stopped here. Most of the untested features are now compared side by side with real Spreeder and fixed. Typecheck is clean, all 30 tests pass, and the work is committed as `2c2f877` in the standalone app.

**Your decisions, applied:**
- **Flow:** keeps the left-to-right reveal.
- **Outside services:** AI Book Summary and AI Generate are removed from the Add Text menu. Look Up Word and the Wordela list stay out.
- **Profile name:** stays "Joshua".
- **LSAT U worktree:** `supabase/functions/mcp/index.ts` is restored and the worktree is clean.

**Fixed to match Spreeder, each checked in our app:**
- **Timing bug:** Spreeder's punctuation pause does not count the plain hyphen (a quirk in its shipped code). Ours did, so we showed "3 minutes remaining" where Spreeder showed "2". Both apps now read "3% - 2 minutes remaining". This also changes word timing.
- **Tags:** this was missing almost entirely. Built:
  - Spreeder's Tags mode, where the menu switches to Back, Create New Tag and the tag list.
  - Each tag's ⋮ menu: 24 tag colours, Edit Tag, and Delete Tag with a confirm.
  - The "Tag <title> as" checklist and the Create New Tag dialog.
- **Bookmarks:** rebuilt from Spreeder's code.
  - The list shows "Page N - Word", the note, and a ⋮ menu with Go to location and Delete.
  - The note dialog saves on its own 5 seconds after you stop typing.
  - The button reads Save for a new bookmark and Update for an existing one.
- **Library:**
  - Card sizes and spacing.
  - The card ⋮ menu size and position.
  - Delete Text confirm, and Edit Image (upload only).
  - In View/Location, clicking a word now moves your place and keeps the panel open, in the library and the reader. Ours used to jump into the reader or close the panel.
  - The collapsed menu is now a narrow 90px icon strip, not hidden.
- **Text screens:** Add To Library puts the cursor in the title and has a 120px Save. Edit Text now has Author and Genre fields.
- **Smaller fixes:** the word pop-up menu sizes, and the bottom bar's narrow-window layout (at 1180px and below the keyboard button hides). Also dialog titles no longer wrap, and the scrollbar is Spreeder's 8px one.

**Not fully verified:**
- **Inferred from Spreeder's code, not seen live:** the filled bookmark list and the filled tag list. Making them live would have meant adding data to your real account.
- **Not checked against Spreeder:**
  - Its Import Document screen (it opens a file picker). Ours works when fed a file directly.
  - The finish alert.
  - Fullscreen (needs a real click).
  - High Contrast and System themes.
  - The shortcuts popover. It only appears in windows wider than 1180px, and your Chrome window is 1036px. Ours works and all the keys work.
- **Small quirk:** pressing ↑ and + in the same instant adds 10 WPM, not 20. Normal separate presses work.

**Side effects on your Spreeder account:** two misclicks opened texts in the reader. That only reordered Recent, and both texts are still at 0% and 3%. Nothing was created, saved or deleted, and I closed the Chrome tab I opened.

**Still to do:** check the items above against Spreeder and look over the Profile screens again. After that comes moving the app into LSAT U. All the measurements are in section 7 of `research/2026-10-13-spreeder-reconstruction-spec.md`. The dev server is still running on port 8100.
